# Coverage waivers and exclusions (Belenos)

The Verilator coverage of `./run_all -cov` (`bin/runcov`) is reported by
`bin/cov_report.py` against this file (format and checks: `bin/cov_waive.py`). A waived
point leaves the denominator and is counted apart, a waiver that matches nothing is
reported as STALE, and `../bin/cov_report.py cov/dats --lint` lists, per entry, what it
matches. Entries are rules: a file, a source pattern (`re/.../` or `/literal/`, never a
line number, so that RTL edits above a point do not move them) and a signal or point
glob, each with its reason. The target is 100 % line, branch and toggle coverage of
what is left.

Classes (the coverage review's): **c**, a Verilator counting artefact (the point exists
but is never counted, whatever the test does); **d**, structural (a lint sink with no
fan-out, a bit or branch constant by construction, or by the value range of its
operands, or a point reachable only outside the documented input contract); **e**, the most significant bits of an event counter that need 2^n events,
and the maximum-configuration bits (DPI source lines, positions and tags beyond about
2 048, `DIM_W` 16 drawing dimensions and DBI windows), covered up to the deployed and HD
geometries. Holes that a test can reach and dead RTL are not waived.

The jitter model of `arv_synchronizer` (`ARV_SYNC_JITTER`, simulation only) is outside
the measurement by a `coverage_off` pragma in arvern-ips, not by a waiver.

## Lint sinks (class d, exclusions)

A `*_unused` wire only collects bits that are otherwise unread, so that the lint sees
every bit used; nothing reads it. These entries exclude the sinks from the toggle metric
whether they toggled or not (`"exclude": true`), one rule per file.

```json
[
{"file": "belenos.v", "at": "re/unused/", "type": "toggle", "sig": "*unused*", "exclude": true, "why": "lint sink (*_unused): a wire that only collects otherwise unread bits for the lint, no fan-out"},
{"file": "belenos_core.v", "at": "re/unused/", "type": "toggle", "sig": "*unused*", "exclude": true, "why": "lint sink (*_unused): a wire that only collects otherwise unread bits for the lint, no fan-out"},
```

## Verilator artefacts (class c)

- A function body's line point is credited for some inlined functions and not for
  others; every function below has live call sites and its result nets toggle.
- A generate `for` that holds only instances and assigns has no procedural code to count.
- An `initial` block's line point is never counted, although the block runs.
- A `?:` whose condition is a parameter gets a branch point that is never counted, even
  where the sweep elaborates both values.

```json
[
{"file": "blns_dpi_scan.v", "at": "re/^\\s*function\\s+\\[[^\\]]*\\]\\s+(bank_of|mux_bank|hblend1|hblend|vblend)\\b/", "type": "line", "sig": "block", "why": "Verilator artefact: the line point of a function body is not credited for every inlined call (the function has live call sites and its result toggles)"},
{"file": "blns_gpu_dec.v", "at": "re/^\\s*function\\b/", "type": "line", "sig": "block", "why": "Verilator artefact: the line point of a function body is not credited for every inlined call (the function has live call sites and its result toggles)"},
{"file": "blns_gpu_px_a.v", "at": "re/^\\s*function\\s+act_f\\b/", "type": "line", "sig": "block", "why": "Verilator artefact: the line point of a function body is not credited for every inlined call (the function has live call sites and its result toggles)"},
{"file": "blns_gpu_px_a.v", "at": "re/^\\s*function\\s+\\[35:0\\]\\s+step_f\\b/", "type": "line", "sig": "block", "why": "Verilator artefact: the line point of a function body is not credited for every inlined call (the function has live call sites and its result toggles)"},
{"file": "blns_gpu_px_fw.v", "at": "re/^\\s*function\\s+\\[17:0\\]\\s+blend_ch\\b/", "type": "line", "sig": "block", "why": "Verilator artefact: the line point of a function body is not credited for every inlined call (the function has live call sites and its result toggles)"},
{"file": "blns_regs.v", "at": "re/^\\s*function\\b/", "type": "line", "sig": "block", "why": "Verilator artefact: the line point of a function body is not credited for every inlined call (the function has live call sites and its result toggles)"},
{"file": "blns_tmds.v", "at": "re/^\\s*function\\b/", "type": "line", "sig": "block", "why": "Verilator artefact: the line point of a function body is not credited for every inlined call (the function has live call sites and its result toggles)"},
{"file": "blns_hdmi.v", "at": "re/^\\s*function\\s+cs_bit\\b/", "type": "line", "sig": "block", "why": "Verilator artefact: the line point of a function body is not credited for every inlined call (the function has live call sites and its result toggles)"},
{"file": "blns_hdmi.v", "at": "re/^\\s*function\\s+\\[55:0\\]\\s+asp_sub\\b/", "type": "line", "sig": "block", "why": "Verilator artefact: the line point of a function body is not credited for every inlined call (the function has live call sites and its result toggles)"},
{"file": "blns_dpi_scan.v", "at": "/for (k = 0; k < NB; k = k + 1)/", "type": "line", "sig": "block", "why": "Verilator artefact: the loop inside the functions bank_of and mux_bank (inlined, live call sites), not credited"},
{"file": "blns_bk_fetch.v", "at": "re/^\\s*for\\s*\\(.*\\bbegin\\s*:\\s*g_/", "type": "line", "sig": "block", "why": "Verilator artefact: a generate for loop holds only instances and assigns, no procedural code to count"},
{"file": "blns_hdmi.v", "at": "re/^\\s*for\\s*\\(.*\\bbegin\\s*:\\s*g_/", "type": "line", "sig": "block", "why": "Verilator artefact: a generate for loop holds only instances and assigns, no procedural code to count"},
{"file": "belenos.v", "at": "re/^\\s*initial\\s+\\$display/", "type": "line", "sig": "block", "why": "Verilator artefact: an elaboration note in an initial block runs, but no initial line point is ever counted"},
{"file": "blns_dbi.v", "at": "/assign cfg_phy_sel     = SPI_EN ?/", "type": "branch", "sig": "cond_then", "why": "Verilator artefact: the condition is a parameter; the point is emitted and never counted although both values are elaborated in the sweep"},
{"file": "blns_dbi.v", "at": "/assign cfg_phy_sel     = SPI_EN ?/", "type": "branch", "sig": "cond_else", "why": "Verilator artefact: the condition is a parameter; the point is emitted and never counted although both values are elaborated in the sweep"},
{"file": "blns_dbi.v", "at": "/assign phy_spi         = SPI_EN ?/", "type": "branch", "sig": "cond_then", "why": "Verilator artefact: the condition is a parameter; the point is emitted and never counted although both values are elaborated in the sweep"},
```

## Structural (class d)

The FSM default arm over unused encodings, the upper half of the 8080 data bus that the
8-bit PHY does not read, and bit 0 of the GPU pixel masks (1 for every legal bpp). One
point is outside the input contract: the HDMI audio frame lost aside (`dlost`), which
needs I2S slots shorter than the 28 pixel clocks the programming model requires
(doc/programming_model.md, section 11.3, Rates). The bits tied to a constant in every
configuration follow, generated.

Not waived, for the record: the raw pixel FIFO's count reaching its depth of 8
(`RD_LAT` + 4 at a total read latency of 4) is covered by `sim_dif0_lut0_rdlat3_rdreg`
(no LUT: the driver pops the FIFO itself). With the LUT, stage C and the output queue
hold part of the credits; a count of 8 is believed reachable there only while stage C
waits on a denied SW-LUT grant, and the sweep does not hit it.

```json
[
{"file": "blns_dbi_engine.v", "at": "re/default\\s*:\\s*state_nxt\\s*=\\s*ST_IDLE/", "type": "line", "sig": "case", "why": "unreachable: the FSM default arm covers the unused state encodings (synthesis safety); every used state has its own arm"},
{"file": "belenos.v", "at": "re/input\\s+wire\\s+\\[15:0\\]\\s+lcd_d_i/", "type": "toggle", "sig": "lcd_d_i[[]8]", "why": "pins of the 16-bit 8080 bus that the 8-bit PHY does not read (lcd_d_15_8_unused)"},
{"file": "belenos.v", "at": "re/input\\s+wire\\s+\\[15:0\\]\\s+lcd_d_i/", "type": "toggle", "sig": "lcd_d_i[[]9]", "why": "pins of the 16-bit 8080 bus that the 8-bit PHY does not read (lcd_d_15_8_unused)"},
{"file": "belenos.v", "at": "re/input\\s+wire\\s+\\[15:0\\]\\s+lcd_d_i/", "type": "toggle", "sig": "lcd_d_i[[]1[0-5]]", "why": "pins of the 16-bit 8080 bus that the 8-bit PHY does not read (lcd_d_15_8_unused)"},
{"file": "blns_dbi.v", "at": "re/input\\s+wire\\s+\\[15:0\\]\\s+lcd_d_i/", "type": "toggle", "sig": "lcd_d_i[[]8]", "why": "pins of the 16-bit 8080 bus that the 8-bit PHY does not read (lcd_d_15_8_unused)"},
{"file": "blns_dbi.v", "at": "re/input\\s+wire\\s+\\[15:0\\]\\s+lcd_d_i/", "type": "toggle", "sig": "lcd_d_i[[]9]", "why": "pins of the 16-bit 8080 bus that the 8-bit PHY does not read (lcd_d_15_8_unused)"},
{"file": "blns_dbi.v", "at": "re/input\\s+wire\\s+\\[15:0\\]\\s+lcd_d_i/", "type": "toggle", "sig": "lcd_d_i[[]1[0-5]]", "why": "pins of the 16-bit 8080 bus that the 8-bit PHY does not read (lcd_d_15_8_unused)"},
{"file": "blns_dbi_8080.v", "at": "re/input\\s+wire\\s+\\[15:0\\]\\s+lcd_d_i/", "type": "toggle", "sig": "lcd_d_i[[]8]", "why": "pins of the 16-bit 8080 bus that the 8-bit PHY does not read (lcd_d_15_8_unused)"},
{"file": "blns_dbi_8080.v", "at": "re/input\\s+wire\\s+\\[15:0\\]\\s+lcd_d_i/", "type": "toggle", "sig": "lcd_d_i[[]9]", "why": "pins of the 16-bit 8080 bus that the 8-bit PHY does not read (lcd_d_15_8_unused)"},
{"file": "blns_dbi_8080.v", "at": "re/input\\s+wire\\s+\\[15:0\\]\\s+lcd_d_i/", "type": "toggle", "sig": "lcd_d_i[[]1[0-5]]", "why": "pins of the 16-bit 8080 bus that the 8-bit PHY does not read (lcd_d_15_8_unused)"},
{"file": "blns_gpu_px_fw.v", "at": "/src_mask16 =/", "type": "toggle", "sig": "src_mask16[[]0]", "why": "constant by construction: bit 0 of the pixel mask is 1 for every legal bpp (an OR of per-bpp terms the constant analysis does not fold)"},
{"file": "blns_gpu_px_fw.v", "at": "/f_rmask8    =/", "type": "toggle", "sig": "f_rmask8[[]0]", "why": "constant by construction: bit 0 of the pixel mask is 1 for every legal bpp (an OR of per-bpp terms the constant analysis does not fold)"},
{"file": "blns_gpu_px_fw.v", "at": "/bpp_mask16  =/", "type": "toggle", "sig": "bpp_mask16[[]0]", "why": "constant by construction: bit 0 of the pixel mask is 1 for every legal bpp (an OR of per-bpp terms the constant analysis does not fold)"},
{"file": "blns_hdmi_i2s.v", "at": "/wire                dlost;/", "type": "toggle", "sig": "dlost", "why": "outside the input contract: a frame waiting aside is lost only when the next I2S slot boundary comes before the sample packet's pop, i.e. with slots shorter than the 28 pixel clocks doc/programming_model.md section 11.3 (HDMI audio, Rates) requires; its one fan-out is the sticky LOST flag (ovf_nxt), and the same boundary clears dv, so the waiting frame is dropped whole and nothing else changes"}
]
```

## Value range (class d)

Bits that the range of their operands keeps at one value, which the constant analysis
does not fold (it sees only the declared widths): a result bounded by its inputs, a
sum of bounded fields, an address bit a guard never lets through, and the no-connect
SPI data input. Each entry says the bound.

```json
{"file": "blns_gpu_setup.v", "at": "/wire [17:0] d_rem;/", "type": "toggle", "sig": "d_rem[[]1[67]]", "why": "value range: d_rem is the remainder of the gradient's restoring division by N <= 4 095 (below N, 12 bits) or, in AA builds, of the line's division |dQ| 2^16 / |dP| (the 12.4 deltas, |dP| <= 65 535; the remainder starts at |dQ| / 2 and stays below |dP|): below 2^16 either way, bits 16-17 are 0"},

{"file": "blns_hdmi_i2s.v", "at": "/wire          [8:0] idx_sum/", "type": "toggle", "sig": "idx_sum[[]8]", "why": "value range: the channel-status position idx (below 192) plus the frames of one write (cnt, 3 bits) is at most 198, so bit 8 of the 9-bit sum is 0"},
{"file": "blns_backend.v", "at": "/wire [OQ_CW:0] oq_cnt_nxt/", "type": "toggle", "sig": "g_lut.oq_cnt_nxt[[]2]", "why": "value range: the RGB888 output queue holds at most OQ_DEPTH = 3 entries (stage C pushes only with oq_slot_ok, count + held < 3): oq_cnt_nxt <= 3, bit 2 is 0"},
{"file": "blns_gpu_px_a.v", "at": "/wire  [3:0] tap_dn;/", "type": "toggle", "sig": "g_bil.tap_dn[[]3]", "why": "value range: the taps are taken lowest first, and tap 3 (x0 + 1, y0 + 1), when the pixel has it, is its last: tap_end clears tap_dn in that cycle, so bit 3 is never set (nor in tap_dn_nxt)"},

{"file": "blns_gpu_px_fw.v", "at": "/wire [14:0] add_b/", "type": "toggle", "sig": "g_blend.add_b[[]1[34]]", "why": "value range: a Pm field plus the rounding constant 128: red and blue at most 7 936 + 128 < 2^13, green at most 16 128 + 128 < 2^14"},
{"file": "blns_gpu_px_l.v", "at": "/wire [19:0] k_a/", "type": "toggle", "sig": "g_e.k_a[[]19]", "why": "value range: a tap weight k <= 1 024 times a' <= 256: k_a <= 2^18, bit 19 is 0"},
{"file": "blns_gpu_px_l.v", "at": "/wire       [10:0] acc_s/", "type": "toggle", "sig": "acc_s[[]10]", "why": "value range: a pixel's tap weights sum to 1 024 and only its taps of nonzero weight are taken, so the sum before its last tap (acc_s; the last tap's sum resets it) is below 1 024: bit 10 is 0"},
{"file": "blns_gpu_px_l.v", "at": "/wire       [11:0] sum_s/", "type": "toggle", "sig": "sum_s[[]11]", "why": "value range: a pixel's tap weights sum to at most 1 024: bit 11 is 0"},
{"file": "blns_gpu_px_l.v", "at": "/wire       [15:0] p_r/", "type": "toggle", "sig": "p_r[[]15]", "why": "value range: e <= 1 024 times a 5-bit channel: at most 31 744 < 2^15 (red, blue), 6-bit green at most 64 512 < 2^16"},
{"file": "blns_gpu_px_l.v", "at": "/wire       [16:0] p_g/", "type": "toggle", "sig": "p_g[[]16]", "why": "value range: e <= 1 024 times a 5-bit channel: at most 31 744 < 2^15 (red, blue), 6-bit green at most 64 512 < 2^16"},

{"file": "blns_gpu_px_tc.v", "at": "/wire [4*VRAM_AW-1:0] e_wa/", "type": "toggle", "sig": "e_wa[[]0]", "why": "value range: entry e of the tap cache holds only words with wa[0] = e[0] (its index is {row parity, wa[0]}), so wa[0] never rises in entries 0 and 2 and, from 0 at reset, never falls in entries 1 and 3"},
{"file": "blns_gpu_px_tc.v", "at": "/wire [4*VRAM_AW-1:0] e_wa/", "type": "toggle", "sig": "e_wa[[]16]", "why": "value range: entry e of the tap cache holds only words with wa[0] = e[0] (its index is {row parity, wa[0]}), so wa[0] never rises in entries 0 and 2 and, from 0 at reset, never falls in entries 1 and 3"},
```

## Counter MSBs and maximum configuration (class e)

The upper bits of an event counter reached only after 2^n events (frames), and the
bits of width-generic counters and comparators that only a
geometry beyond the deployed and HD ones reaches (DPI sources taller or wider than
about 2 048, `DIM_W` 16 drawing and DBI windows, the immediate-source index of a
rectangle). They are covered up to the deployed and HD geometries; their upper bits
are the same logic as the exercised low bits.
